command "aprs.send" "Send an APRS message" "Sends one numbered message under the callsign of the station. command "aprs.send" "Send an APRS message" "Sends one numbered message under the callsign of the station. It goes again after 30, 60, 120 and 240 s until the addressee answers, within the duty limit; after the fifth transmission without an answer it fails. The column `state` of its row turns to delivered when the addressee acknowledges it. A numbered message to the station is acknowledged once per number in 30 s." transmits "text"

command "aprs.send" "Send an APRS message" "Sends one numbered message under the callsign of the station. It goes again after 30, 60, 120 and 240 s until the addressee answers, within the duty limit; after the fifth transmission without an answer it fails. The column `state` of its row turns to delivered when the addressee acknowledges it. A numbered message to the station is acknowledged once per number in 30 s." transmits "text"
